Motivation: 标准自注意力机制在时间序列预测中存在关于回看长度的二次时间和内存复杂度，限制了其在资源受限或高吞吐量预测系统中的应用；通过分析发现时间序列预测中的自注意力图通常包含冗余模式，这与真实世界时间序列中重复的时间模式和相对稳定的时间相关性有关。

Method: 提出自门控注意力(SGA)，采用共享可学习矩阵和输入依赖残差组件来表示注意力分数，共享矩阵捕获常见注意力模式，残差组件捕获输入依赖变化，避免标准注意力计算中的查询和键投影，实现关于回看长度的线性时间和分数矩阵内存复杂度。

Result: 在涵盖电力、金融、天气、医疗监测、人类活动和气候记录的九个公开真实世界数据集上，SGA在提升推理效率的同时，与最先进的注意力机制相比保持竞争力的预测性能。

Conclusion: SGA作为一种即插即用的注意力机制，成功解决了标准自注意力在时间序列预测中的效率瓶颈，为面向部署的应用提供了有效证据。

Abstract: Transformer architectures have shown strong potential in time series forecasting, where multi-head self-attention is widely used to capture temporal dependencies across historical timestamps. However, standard self-attention has quadratic time and memory complexity with respect to the look-back length. This cost may limit its use in resource-constrained or high-throughput forecasting systems, where fast and memory-efficient inference is important. Through qualitative and quantitative analyses, we observe that self-attention maps in time series forecasting often contain redundant patterns across different timestamps. This phenomenon can be related to the repeated temporal patterns and relatively stable temporal correlations in many real-world time series. Motivated by this observation, we propose Self-Gating Attention (SGA), a plug-and-play attention mechanism that represents the attention score with a shared learnable matrix and an input-dependent residual component. The shared matrix captures common attention patterns, while the residual component captures input-dependent variations. In this way, SGA avoids the query and key projections used in standard attention score computation, leading to linear time and score-matrix memory complexity with respect to the look-back length. We integrate SGA into several forecasting backbones and compare it with standard self-attention and lightweight attention variants on nine publicly available real-world datasets covering electricity, finance, weather, medical monitoring, human activity, and climate records. The results show that SGA improves inference efficiency on public benchmarks while maintaining competitive forecasting performance against state-of-the-art attention mechanisms. These benchmark results provide deployment-oriented evidence.

</details>


### [179] [DecompRL: Solving Harder Problems by Learning Modular Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02390)
*Juliette Decugis,Fabian Gloeckle,Francis Bach,Taco Cohen,Gabriel Synnaeve*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出DecompRL算法，通过学习将问题分解为可独立求解的子模块并重新组合，大幅降低GPU成本，解决了大语言模型在基础策略正确解概率接近零时无法通过采样或强化学习克服的瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法存在局限性：重复采样虽可扩展测试时计算但GPU成本随尝试次数线性增长；基于可验证奖励的强化学习虽能提高单次准确率但牺牲样本多样性。更关键的是，当基础策略产生正确解的概率接近零时，搜索空间过大导致采样和梯度信号均无法奏效。

Method: 提出DecompRL算法，不同于加大采样力度，而是将问题分解为更小、可独立求解的子函数，其实现可以重新组合。该算法通过强化学习显式学习分解和实现层次化代码结构。将n个模块的k个实现重新组合可产生最多k^n个候选解，将瓶颈从GPU推理转移到廉价的CPU评估。

Result: 在LiveCodeBench和CodeContests数据集上（使用Qwen 2.5 7B和Code World Model 32B），DecompRL在每问题超过10^5 tokens时超越标准和多样性优化的强化学习基线；GPU token成本降低约50倍；能够解决标准生成无法达到的问题。

Conclusion: 通过模块化分解与重组的方法，DecompRL成功突破了传统采样和强化学习方法在搜索空间过大时的局限性，为提升大语言模型问题求解能力提供了新的技术路径，在代码生成任务上取得显著性能提升和成本节约。

Abstract: How can Large Language Models (LLMs) solve problems they currently cannot? Repeated sampling scales test-time compute but GPU cost grows linearly with attempts, while reinforcement learning (RL) with verifiable rewards improves single-attempt accuracy at the expense of sample diversity. Both strategies ultimately fail when the base policy has near-zero probability of producing a correct solution: no amount of sampling or gradient signal can overcome a search space that is simply too large. We take a different approach: rather than sampling harder, we make the task easier by decomposing problems into smaller, independently solvable sub-functions whose implementations can be recombined. Since off-the-shelf models are not trained for this modular generation, we introduce DecompRL, an RL algorithm that explicitly learns to decompose and implement hierarchical code structures. Recombining $k$ implementations of $n$ modules yields up to $k^{n}$ candidate solutions, shifting the bottleneck from GPU inference to cheap CPU evaluation and cutting GPU token cost by $\sim$50$\times$. On LiveCodeBench and CodeContests (Qwen~2.5~7B, Code World Model~32B), DecompRL outperforms standard and diversity-optimized RL baselines beyond $10^5$ tokens per problem, solving problems that standard generation cannot reach.

</details>


### [180] [Neuron-Aware Active Few-Shot Learning for LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02423)
*Zhuowei Chen,Liwei Chen,Christian Schunn,Raquel Coelho,Xiang Lorraine Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了NeuFS框架，利用神经元激活模式替代输出级信号进行主动少样本学习的样本选择，显著提升了LLMs在专业领域的适应性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多数主动少样本学习方法依赖输出级信号（如预测熵或基于外部嵌入的语义相似度）进行样本识别，忽略了能精确指出特定知识空白的模型内部动态信息。

Method: 提出NeuFS框架，核心创新包括：(1) 利用神经元激活模式直接表征样本；(2) 设计双准则选择策略，一方面通过神经元模式确保少样本的多样性以实现更广的示例覆盖，另一方面通过量化神经元共识优先识别LLMs易产生幻觉的困难样本。

Result: 在三个数据集上的实验表明，NeuFS在推理和文本分类任务上均表现优异，超越了现有的AFSL基线方法；消融研究证明内部神经元激活比外部嵌入提供了更有效、更原则性的选择信号。

Conclusion: 将样本选择范式从输出级代理信号转向模型内部动态具有显著优势，NeuFS验证了利用神经元信息进行主动少样本学习的有效性和优越性。

Abstract: Active Few-Shot Learning (AFSL) adapts LLMs to specialized domains by identifying the most valuable unlabeled samples for annotation and use as few-shot demonstrations, effectively reducing human annotation costs while promoting high performance. However, existing methods typically rely on output-level signals for sample identification, such as predictive entropy or semantic similarities with test-time data based on external embeddings, which often overlook models' internal dynamics, which could pinpoint specific knowledge gaps. To bridge this gap, we propose NeuFS, a Neuron-Aware Active Few-Shot Learning framework that shifts the selection paradigm from output-level proxies to models' internal dynamics. NeuFS utilizes neuron activation patterns to represent sample directly, and includes a dual-criteria selection strategy that: (1) ensures few-shot sample diversity with neuron patterns for broader example coverage, while (2) prioritizing on identifying informative and challenging few-shot samples LLMs tend to hallucinate by quantifying neuron consensus. Experiments on three datasets demonstrate that NeuFS excels in both reasoning and text classification tasks, outperforming existing AFSL baselines. Ablation studies further highlight that internal neuron activations provide a more principled and effective selection signal than external embeddings, validating the superiority of the proposed NeuFS.

</details>


### [181] [QFedAgent: Quantum-Enhanced Personalized Federated Learning for Multi-Agent Activity Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02426)
*Quoc Bao Phan,Tuy Tan Nguyen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了QFedAgent，一个混合量子-经典个性化联邦学习框架，用于多智能体活动识别。通过变分量子电路融合模块，实现了相比经典融合方法约10倍的参数减少，同时保持了97.7%的测试准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习适用于隐私敏感的机器人感知应用，但多智能体系统产生的异构和非独立同分布（non-IID）多模态传感器流会导致传统联邦学习算法性能下降，同时经典融合模块引入了大量参数开销和通信成本。

Method: 提出了QFedAgent混合量子-经典个性化联邦学习框架，集成了变分量子电路融合模块，通过量子态编码和纠缠来建模加速度计-陀螺仪的交互，仅需72个量子旋转参数，而基于多层感知机的经典融合需要33K参数。

Result: 在OPPORTUNITY数据集上基于主体的non-IID分区实验中，实现了97.7%的平均测试准确率，证明了参数高效的量子融合方法能够与传统联邦基线方法保持竞争力，同时实现了约10倍的总参数减少。

Conclusion: 参数高效的量子融合方法在多智能体活动识别任务中具有可行性，能够在大幅减少参数和通信成本的同时，保持与常规联邦基线方法相当的性能水平。

Abstract: Federated learning (FL) enables collaborative model training across distributed devices without sharing raw data, making it suitable for privacy-sensitive robotic sensing applications. However, multi-agent systems generate heterogeneous and non-independent and identically distributed (non-IID) multimodal sensor streams that degrade conventional FL algorithms, while classical fusion modules introduce substantial parameter overhead and communication cost. This paper proposes QFedAgent, a hybrid quantum-classical personalized FL framework for multi-agent activity recognition. The approach integrates a variational quantum circuit fusion module that models accelerometer--gyroscope interactions through quantum state encoding and entanglement, requiring only 72 quantum rotation parameters versus 33K in classical multi-layer perceptron-based fusion, achieving approximately 10x total parameter reduction. Experiments on the OPPORTUNITY dataset under subject-based non-IID partitions demonstrate 97.7% mean test accuracy, confirming that parameter-efficient quantum fusion remains competitive with conventional federated baselines.

</details>


### [182] [Extreme Adaptive Transformer for Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02437)
*Sanjeev Shrestha,Hui Liu,Yifan Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Exformer模型，通过引入局部、步长和极端三种稀疏注意力组件，解决了包含罕见极端事件的时间序列预测难题，在四个水文数据集上取得了最优的3天预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于Transformer的时间序列预测模型通常对所有时间点进行均匀处理，难以有效捕捉罕见的极端事件模式。在水文预测中，径流分布通常高度偏斜，极端峰值对洪水监测、水资源管理和预警系统具有重要影响，因此亟需能够显式建模极端事件的预测方法。

Method: 提出Extreme-Adaptive Transformer (Exformer)框架，设计了包含三个稀疏组件的极端自适应注意力机制：(1)局部组件捕捉短期时间依赖；(2)步长组件捕捉周期性时间依赖；(3)极端组件选择性地建模正常和极端径流模式之间的事件感知依赖关系。

Result: 在四个真实水文径流数据集上的实验表明，Exformer在3天预测任务上优于现有最先进的基线模型，证明了显式引入极端感知注意力机制的有效性。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，通过显式纳入极端感知注意力机制，可以有效提升Transformer模型在包含罕见但重要极端事件的非平衡时间序列上的预测能力，为极端事件预测提供了新的技术路径。

Abstract: Time series forecasting remains challenging when the underlying data contain rare but critical extreme events. This issue is particularly important in hydrologic forecasting, where streamflow distributions are often highly skewed and extreme peaks can have substantial impacts on flood monitoring, water resource management, and early warning systems. Although Transformer-based forecasting models have achieved strong performance by modeling long-range temporal dependencies, they typically treat all time points uniformly and may therefore underrepresent rare extreme patterns. In this paper, we propose the Extreme-Adaptive Transformer (Exformer), a forecasting framework designed to explicitly model temporal dependencies involving both normal and extreme events. Exformer introduces an extreme-adaptive attention mechanism composed of three sparse components: Local, Stride, and Extreme. The Local and Stride components capture short-term and periodic temporal dependencies, respectively, while the Extreme component selectively models event-aware dependencies between normal and extreme streamflow patterns. Experiments on four real-world hydrologic streamflow datasets show that Exformer achieves superior 3-day forecasting performance compared with state-of-the-art baselines. Our findings demonstrate that explicitly incorporating extreme-aware attention improves the forecasting capacity of Transformer models on imbalanced time series with rare but consequential events.

</details>


### [183] [Understanding the Robustness of Distributed Self-Supervised Learning Frameworks Against Non-IID Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02447)
*Xuanyu Chen,Nan Yang,Shuai Wang,Dong Yuan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对分布式自监督学习（D-SSL）在非独立同分布（non-IID）数据异构环境下的鲁棒性进行了严格的理论分析，证明了掩码图像建模（MIM）比对比学习（CL）在异构数据下更具鲁棒性，联邦学习的鲁棒性不低于去中心化学习，并提出了基于理论的MAR损失函数来验证理论的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布式自监督学习（D-SSL）方法虽然能够利用大量分散的无标签数据，但面临数据异构性的关键挑战。目前对不同D-SSL框架如何应对数据异构性的理论理解非常有限，缺乏对D-SSL在非独立同分布设置下鲁棒性的深入理论分析。

Method: 本文对D-SSL框架在非独立同分布设置下的鲁棒性进行严格的理论分析，比较了掩码图像建模（MIM）与对比学习（CL）两种方法的抗异构能力，分析了网络平均连接性与鲁棒性的关系，比较了联邦学习与去中心化学习的鲁棒性。基于理论发现，提出了MAR损失函数，该损失在MIM目标基础上增加了局部到全局的对齐正则化。

Result: 理论分析表明：（1）使用掩码图像建模（MIM）的预训练在异构数据下本质上比对比学习（CL）更具鲁棒性；（2）分布式SSL的鲁棒性随网络平均连接度的增加而提升，这意味着联邦学习的鲁棒性不低于去中心化学习。实验结果在不同模型架构和分布式设置下验证了理论见解，同时证明了MAR损失函数的有效性。

Conclusion: 本研究为分布式自监督学习在数据异构环境下的鲁棒性提供了坚实的理论基础，揭示了MIM方法相比CL方法的优势以及网络拓扑结构对鲁棒性的影响，为未来D-SSL算法的设计提供了重要的理论指导。提出的MAR损失函数展示了如何将理论分析应用于实际算法改进。

Abstract: Recent research has introduced distributed self-supervised learning (D-SSL) approaches to leverage vast amounts of unlabeled decentralized data. However, D-SSL faces the critical challenge of data heterogeneity, and there is limited theoretical understanding of how different D-SSL frameworks respond to this challenge. To fill this gap, we present a rigorous theoretical analysis of the robustness of D-SSL frameworks under non-IID (non-independent and identically distributed) settings. Our results show that pre-training with Masked Image Modeling (MIM) is inherently more robust to heterogeneous data than Contrastive Learning (CL), and that the robustness of decentralized SSL increases with average network connectivity, implying that federated learning (FL) is no less robust than decentralized learning (DecL). These findings provide a solid theoretical foundation for guiding the design of future D-SSL algorithms. To further illustrate the practical implications of our theory, we introduce MAR loss, a refinement of the MIM objective with local-to-global alignment regularization. Extensive experiments across model architectures and distributed settings validate our theoretical insights, and additionally confirm the effectiveness of MAR loss as an application of our analysis.

</details>


### [184] [Neuron-Aware Data Selection for Annotation-Free LLM Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02460)
*Zhuowei Chen,Xiang Lorraine Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了Neuron-OPSD框架，利用神经元激活指导训练数据选择和教师上下文构建，通过在策略自蒸馏实现无需标注的自我进化，在专业领域任务上取得更好性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 后训练大语言模型在专业领域面临专家标注成本高的问题。现有的无标注自我进化方法存在缺陷：基于SFT和GRPO的方法会导致域外性能下降，而基于奖励的在策略强化学习会增大校准误差。

Method: 提出神经元在策略自蒸馏（Neuron-OPSD）框架，这是一种以数据为中心的无标注自蒸馏方法。利用模型内部神经元激活来指导训练数据选择和教师上下文构建，通过从教师分布进行在策略蒸馏来训练模型，全程无需真实标签。

Result: 在专业领域基准测试中，Neuron-OPSD提高了域内任务性能，同时保持了跨域泛化能力，并缓解了校准崩溃问题，优于先前的无标注基线方法。

Conclusion: 该框架特别适用于在线交互或外部监督成本高昂或不可行的场景，概念上有别于依赖已记录奖励标注轨迹的离线强化学习方法，为专业领域LLM后训练提供了有效的无标注解决方案。

Abstract: Post-training large language models (LLMs) without real-world interaction feedback or human-labeled supervision remains challenging, particularly in specialized domains where expert annotations are costly to obtain. Recent annotation-free self-evolution methods address this by using the model's own outputs as supervision signals, constructing a teacher via additional context and aggregating predictions across multiple rollouts through majority voting to produce pseudo-labels. However, these approaches are not without drawbacks: SFT- and GRPO-based variants suffer out-of-domain performance degradation, while reward-based on-policy RL inflates calibration error. In this paper, we propose Neuron On-Policy Self-Distillation (Neuron-OPSD), a data-centric framework for annotation-free self-distillation that leverages internal neuron activations to guide both training-data selection and teacher context construction. The model is then trained via on-policy distillation from the teacher distribution, requiring no ground-truth labels at any stage. Across specialized-domain benchmarks, Neuron-OPSD improves in-domain task performance while preserving cross-domain generalization and mitigating calibration collapse over prior annotation-free baselines. This framework is particularly relevant to settings where online interaction or external supervision is costly or infeasible, and is conceptually distinct from offline RL approaches that rely on logged, reward-labeled trajectories.

</details>


### [185] [Beyond Adam: SOAP and Muon for Faster, Label-Efficient Training of Machine Learning Interatomic Potentials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02499)
*Gil Harari,Yoel Zimmermann,Ola Tangen Kulseng,Laura Zichi,Chuin Wei Tan,Marc L. Descoteaux,Boris Kozinsky*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了矩阵结构优化器在机器学习原子间势（MLIPs）训练中的表现，发现SOAP和SOAP-Muon优化器在收敛速度和最终精度上显著优于广泛使用的Adam优化器，揭示了优化器选择是MLIPs中被忽视但影响重大的设计维度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管机器学习原子间势领域在架构和数据集方面取得了显著进展，但优化器的选择一直未被充分探索，社区普遍默认使用Adam及其变体。作者旨在系统评估一类新兴的矩阵结构优化器在MLIPs训练中的性能表现，填补这一研究空白。

Method: 研究实现了三类矩阵结构优化器（Muon、SOAP以及混合SOAP-Muon），并在NequIP和Allegro两种主流MLIP模型上进行了系统性训练对比实验，特别评估了部分力监督条件下的优化器性能。

Result: 实验结果表明：SOAP和SOAP-Muon优化器在收敛速度和最终精度方面始终表现优异且稳健，显著超越Adam；Muon相对于Adam仅提供部分改进；在部分力监督场景下，性能提升尤为明显。

Conclusion: 优化器选择是MLIPs设计中一个重要但被忽视的维度。SOAP和SOAP-Muon作为稳健强力的优化方法值得在MLIPs训练中推广应用。该发现为提升MLIPs训练效率和精度提供了新的技术途径。

Abstract: Machine learning interatomic potentials (MLIPs) have become a hallmark of AI for scientific simulation. While efforts on new architectures and datasets have led to increasingly accurate and general models, the choice of optimizer for training has largely remained unexplored, defaulting to Adam and its variants in the community. Here, we implement and systematically compare a class of recently proposed matrix-structured optimizers, including Muon, SOAP, and the hybrid SOAP-Muon, for training NequIP and Allegro MLIP models. We find that these optimizers can substantially outperform Adam in both convergence speed and final accuracy. SOAP and SOAP-Muon emerge as robust and consistently strong methods, while Muon only provides partial gains relative to Adam. The improvements are particularly pronounced under partial force supervision. Our results indicate that optimizer choice is an overlooked yet impactful design axis for MLIPs.

</details>


### [186] [DemoPSD: Disagreement-Modulated Policy Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02502)
*Yunhe Li,Hao Shi,Wenhao Liu,Mengzhe Ruan,Hanxu Hou,Zhongxiang Dai,Shuang Qiu,Linqi Song*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DemoPSD通过选择性采用教师指导，使用逆KL重心目标来平衡学习与探索，有效解决了特权信息泄漏问题，在科学领域推理任务上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的在线策略自蒸馏（OPSD）方法存在三个关键问题：(1) 教师的密集token级监督条件化于特权信息，导致对域内模式的过拟合；(2) 抑制探索能力并损害跨域泛化；(3) 特权信息泄漏问题，即学生在测试时编码了依赖答案的捷径信息，而这些信息在测试时不可用。

Method: 提出DemoPSD框架，采用选择性教师指导采纳策略。核心机制：(1) 将学生引导向逆KL重心目标——教师与学生分布的加权几何组合；(2) 自然平衡从教师学习与保留学生自身推理能力；(3) 测量分布差异并利用该差异在每个token位置自适应控制混合权重。该方法理论上证明了能实现特权信息泄漏缓解和探索能力保持。

Result: 在SciKnowEval四个科学领域的广泛实验中，DemoPSD在性能上优于GRPO和SDPO基线方法，同时保持了更高的训练熵，并在分布外GPQA基准测试中表现出鲁棒的泛化能力。理论证明实现了泄漏缓解和探索保持两个关键性质。

Conclusion: DemoPSD成功解决了OPSD中的特权信息泄漏问题，通过逆KL重心目标实现了教师指导与学生自主推理能力的平衡，为大型语言模型的推理训练提供了一个更有效、泛化性更强的方法。

Abstract: On-policy self-distillation (OPSD) has emerged as a practical method for training large language models (LLMs) to reason, where a single model acts as both the teacher and the student with different levels of information access. However, recent studies have found that the teacher's dense token-level supervision, conditioned on privileged information, can lead to overfitting to in-domain patterns, suppress exploration, and hurt cross-domain generalization, while also introducing a more fundamental issue: *privileged information leakage*, where the student encodes answer-dependent shortcuts that are unavailable at test time. We introduce **DemoPSD**, a novel framework that resolves such problems through the idea of *selective adoption of teacher guidance*. Instead of fitting the full teacher distribution, DemoPSD steers the student toward a *reverse-KL barycenter target*, a weighted geometric combination of the teacher and student distributions, that naturally balances learning from the teacher with preserving the student's own reasoning capacity. We measure the difference between their distributions and use such a discrepancy to adaptively control the blending at each token position. We provably show that DemoPSD achieves **(1)** *leakage attenuation*, i.e., effective mitigation of privileged information leakage; and **(2)** *exploration preservation*, i.e., preservation of exploration capacity under dense token-level distillation. Extensive experiments on SciKnowEval across four scientific fields show that DemoPSD outperforms both GRPO and SDPO while maintaining higher training entropy and robustly generalizing to out-of-distribution GPQA benchmarks.

</details>


### [187] [Program-as-Weights: A Programming Paradigm for Fuzzy Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02512)
*Wentao Zhang,Liliana Hotsko,Woojeong Kim,Pengyu Nie,Stuart Shieber,Yuntian Deng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了模糊函数编程范式和PAW系统，将自然语言函数规范编译为紧凑的本地可执行神经产物，在大幅降低推理成本的同时匹配大规模模型的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多日常编程任务（如日志警报、JSON修复、搜索排序）难以用规则实现，现有方案依赖大型语言模型API，存在本地性差、不可复现、成本高的问题。

Method: 提出模糊函数编程范式，实现为PAW系统：在1000万样本的FuzzyBench数据集上训练4B参数编译器，为冻结的轻量级解释器生成参数高效适配器。将大模型重构为工具构建器而非每次输入的问题求解器。

Result: 0.6B参数的Qwen3解释器执行PAW程序，性能匹配直接提示32B参数的Qwen3，推理内存仅为其1/50，在MacBook M3上运行速度30 tokens/s。

Conclusion: PAW成功将大模型能力压缩到小型可复用产物中，实现了每函数定义调用一次大模型，后续应用调用快速且离线，显著降低了计算成本和依赖。

Abstract: Many everyday programming tasks resist clean rule-based implementation, such as alerting on important log lines, repairing malformed JSON, or ranking search results by intent, and are increasingly outsourced to large language model APIs at the cost of locality, reproducibility, and price. We propose fuzzy-function programming: compiling such a function from a natural-language specification into a compact, locally-executable neural artifact. We instantiate this paradigm with Program-as-Weights (PAW), in which a 4B compiler trained on FuzzyBench, a 10M-example dataset we release, emits parameter-efficient adapters for a frozen, lightweight interpreter. A 0.6B Qwen3 interpreter executing PAW programs matches the performance of direct prompting of Qwen3-32B, while using roughly one fiftieth of the inference memory and running at 30 tokens/s on a MacBook M3. PAW reframes the foundation model from a per-input problem solver into a tool builder: invoked once per function definition, it produces a small reusable artifact whose subsequent calls per function application are cheap and offline.

</details>


<div id='cs.AR'></div>

# cs.AR [[Back]](#toc)

### [188] [Field-Deployable RF Capture System for Indoor, Outdoor, and Foliage Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01368)
*Lawrence Obiuwevwi,Krzysztof J. Rechowicz,Vikas Ashok,Sachin Shetty,Peter B. Foytik,Jared Cochran,Jeff Bobrow,Sampath Jayarathna*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文开发了一种便携式、低成本的电池供电射频采集系统，成功实现了高达20 Msps的连续IQ数据记录，并在多种实际环境（茂密植被、城市户外、室内办公室）中验证了其在频谱特征分析方面的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的射频测量平台多为实验室级别、成本高昂或依赖固定基础设施，难以满足快速、分布式或长期现场测量的需求。因此，迫切需要一种紧凑、便携且经济高效的解决方案，以实现对未授权ISM/WiFi频段、授权中频段及新兴无线部署的可靠、可重复的射频测量。

Method: 构建了一个集成的紧凑型电池供电射频采集系统，核心组件包括HackRF One软件无线电、Raspberry Pi 5、GNSS接收器、稳压电池供电和高速固态存储。该平台以SigMF格式记录连续IQ数据，采样率最高达20 Msps，并为每个数据段附带位置和时间元数据，以支持可重复的频谱分析。研究在2.45 GHz频段进行了野外实验，涵盖茂密植被、城市户外和室内办公室三种环境。

Result: 实验揭示了不同环境的独特传播特征：植被环境测量值接近噪声底（-76至-82 dBFS），频谱结构有限，符合强树冠衰减特征；城市测量显示30 dB动态范围内的多径活动、重叠WiFi信道及频繁的ISM频段干扰；室内测量显示主导WiFi信道，相对于户外条件存在约20-25 dB的建筑进入损耗，以及由结构反射导致的干扰底噪提高8-10 dB。系统实现了75-85 MB/s的稳定写入吞吐量，无丢包或缓冲欠载，GNSS同步时间低于1秒，定位精度达米级。

Conclusion: 研究结果证明，便携式、成本效益高的SDR平台能够生成高保真、带地理标签的IQ数据集，适用于频谱特征分析、干扰分析、无线电环境测绘以及环境感知无线研究，为实际环境下的可靠射频测量提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Reliable and reproducible radio-frequency (RF) measurements in real-world environments are essential for characterizing spectrum behavior across unlicensed ISM and WiFi bands, licensed mid-band allocations, and emerging next-generation wireless deployments. Existing measurement platforms are often laboratory-grade, cost-prohibitive, or dependent on fixed infrastructure, limiting their practicality for rapid, distributed, or long-duration field campaigns. This paper presents a compact, battery-powered RF capture system integrating a HackRF One software-defined radio, Raspberry Pi 5, GNSS receiver, regulated battery supply, and high-speed solid-state storage. The platform records continuous IQ data at up to 20 Msps in SigMF format with per-segment location and timing metadata for reproducible spectrum analysis. Field experiments at 2.45 GHz in dense foliage, urban outdoor, and indoor office environments reveal distinct propagation signatures. Foliage measurements remain near the noise floor at -76 to -82 dBFS with limited spectral structure, consistent with strong canopy attenuation. Urban measurements show multipath activity across a 30 dB dynamic range, overlapping WiFi channels, and frequent ISM-band interference. Indoor measurements show dominant WiFi channels, an estimated 20 to 25 dB building entry loss relative to outdoor conditions, and an 8 to 10 dB higher interference floor caused by structural reflections. The system sustained 75 to 85 MB/s write throughput with no dropped samples or buffer underruns, while GNSS synchronization remained below one second with meter-level positioning. These results show that a portable, cost-effective SDR platform can produce high-fidelity, geotagged IQ datasets for spectrum characterization, interference analysis, radio environment mapping, and environment-aware wireless research.

</details>


### [189] [Physically-Aware Preemptive Virtual Channels for Deadlock-Free AXI Networks-on-Chip](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01430)
*Lorenzo Leone,Luca Colagrande,Luca Benini*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出Preemptive VCs架构，在保证AXI4系统无死锁的前提下，相比多平面设计节省高达76%的链路资源，路由器面积开销仅增加3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着众核SoC规模扩展，NoC需维持高内存带宽并保持无死锁。AXI4系统中读写流量间的协议级依赖会在网络端点形成循环等待，解耦这些流量类别虽能避免依赖，但多平面NoC会复制宽物理链路增加路由压力，传统虚拟通道路由器则带来控制复杂度、面积和时序开销。

Method: 针对现代宽链路NoC重新评估死锁避免的权衡，对比四种AXI4流量类别分离方案：一个多平面基线和三个轻量级VC设计。提出Preemptive VCs架构，这是一种物理感知的设计方案。

Result: Preemptive VCs设计相比多平面方案可节省高达76%的链路资源，频率性能相当，路由器面积开销仅增加3%。

Conclusion: 在现代宽链路NoC中，Preemptive VCs提供了比传统多平面设计更优的资源效率，实现了链路资源节省与低面积开销的良好平衡，是AXI4系统流量分离的有效解决方案。

Abstract: As many-core Systems-on-Chip (SoCs) continue to scale, Networks-on-Chip (NoCs) must sustain increasingly high memory bandwidth while preserving deadlock freedom. In AXI4 systems, protocol-level dependencies between read and write traffic can create circular waits at the network endpoints, even when the routing algorithm itself is deadlock-free. Decoupling these traffic classes avoids such dependencies, but exposes a key implementation trade-off: multiplane NoCs duplicate wide physical links and increase routing pressure, whereas conventional Virtual Channel (VC) routers add substantial control complexity, area, and timing overhead. This work revisits this trade-off for modern wide-link NoCs. We evaluate four deadlock-free AXI4 traffic-class separation schemes: a multiplane baseline and three lightweight VC-based designs. Among these designs, we propose Preemptive VCs, a physically-aware architecture that can save up to 76% of link resources with comparable frequency and only 3% router area overhead relative to the multiplane design.

</details>


### [190] [MxGLUT: A Reconfigurable LUT-Centric Broadcast Dataflow Accelerator for Mixed-Precision GEMM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01607)
*Weiyu Zhou,Chen Ding,Mingyuan Liu,Liangyu Gan,Yukun Feng,Hao Jia,Haoming Chu,Lirong Zheng,Ning Ma,Yuxiang Huan*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 提出MxGLUT加速器，通过统一的LUT计算机制支持FP8-INT4和FP8-FP8混合精度计算，采用可重构数据流优化prefill和decode阶段，显著降低面积和能耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LUT加速器仅支持FP8-INT4计算，仍需独立的浮点数据通路处理注意力GEMM操作，导致硬件冗余和非统一的混合精度执行；此外，静态数据流无法匹配prefill和decode两个阶段的差异化特征。

Method: 提出MxGLUT加速器，基于混合精度LUT处理单元(MxLPE)构建可重构LUT中心广播(RLB)数据流；采用统一LUT执行框架，将FP8-INT4和FP8-FP8 GEMM统一到单一LUT计算机制下，无需专用FP乘法器；RB数据流在prefill阶段局部化重量级部分和累加，在decode阶段利用权重重用。

Result: 在UMC 28nm CMOS 200MHz下，乘法器面积减少56.92%，FP8-INT4和FP8-FP8模式下功耗分别降低77.07%和78.35%；加速器级面积效率0.492 TFLOPS/mm²，能效11.58 TFLOPS/W；相比FP8-INT4基线，添加原生FP8-FP8支持仅导致面积和能效分别下降2.57%和3.34%；在Llama模型上实现高达2.16×和1.49×的延迟加速，prefill和decode阶段归一化能耗降至0.44×和0.71×，困惑度增加最多1.70%。

Conclusion: MxGLUT成功实现了统一的混合精度LUT计算框架，通过消除专用FP数据通路显著降低了硬件开销，同时可重构数据流有效适配了LLM推理两阶段的差异化需求，在保持高精度的同时实现了显著的性能和能效提升。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) inference suffers from growing inefficiency across the prefill and decode phases, especially under weight-only quantization, where activations remain in FP8 while weights are compressed to low-bit integers. Existing LUT-based accelerators mainly target FP8-INT4 computation and still rely on separate floating-point (FP) datapaths for attention GEMM operations, leading to redundant hardware and non-unified mixed-precision execution. Moreover, their static dataflows are poorly matched to the distinct prefill and decode phases. To address these challenges, we propose MxGLUT, a reconfigurable LUT-centric broadcast (RLB) dataflow accelerator built on mixed-precision LUT-based processing elements (MxLPEs). Guided by a unified LUT-based execution framework, MxGLUT organizes both FP8-INT4 and FP8-FP8 GEMMs under a single LUT-based compute mechanism without dedicated FP multipliers or additional FP datapaths, and further adopts the RLB dataflow that localizes heavy partial-sum accumulation during the prefill phase and exploits weight reuse in the decode phase. Synthesized in UMC $28\,\mathrm{nm}$ CMOS at $200~\mathrm{MHz}$, MxGLUT reduces multiplier area by up to $56.92\%$ and power by up to $77.07\%$ and $78.35\%$ in FP8-INT4 and FP8-FP8 modes, respectively. At the accelerator level, MxGLUT achieves an area efficiency of $0.492~\mathrm{TFLOPS/mm^2}$ and an energy efficiency of $11.58~\mathrm{TFLOPS/W}$, while adding native FP8-FP8 support incurs only $2.57\%$ and $3.34\%$ reductions in area and energy efficiency, respectively, relative to the FP8-INT4-only FIGLUT baseline. Across the Llama family, MxGLUT achieves up to $2.16\times$ and $1.49\times$ latency speedup, and reduces normalized energy to $0.44\times$ and $0.71\times$ in prefill and decode, respectively, with at most $1.70\%$ perplexity increase.

</details>


### [191] [3DLS: A 3D Logic-Stacked Architecture for Disaggregated LLM Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01617)
*Jaehun Lee,In-Jun Jung,Joo-Young Kim*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出3DLS，一种逻辑对逻辑的3D堆叠芯粒架构，通过垂直互连传输KV缓存，将解码侧TP集合通信隔离在横向D2D互连上，有效解决传统架构中的流量竞争问题。相比共享平面基线，吞吐量提升至1.49倍，端到端延迟降低60.2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在传统的2D/2.5D芯粒架构中，逐层的预填充到解码KV缓存传输与解码侧张量并行集合通信共享同一横向裸片到裸片互连，在解码关键路径上产生混合流量竞争，导致通信延迟增加、令牌生成间隔延长以及端到端服务性能下降。

Method: 提出3DLS架构，采用逻辑对逻辑的3D堆叠芯粒设计，通过垂直互连路由KV缓存传输，同时将解码侧张量并行集合通信保留在横向D2D互连结构上，实现不同流量类别在物理层面的隔离。

Result: 相比共享结构的平面基线，3DLS实现了最高1.49倍的吞吐量提升和60.2%的端到端延迟降低；相比负载感知的优先级管理平面基线，仍实现了最高1.17倍的吞吐量提升和31.4%的端到端延迟降低。

Conclusion: 物理隔离是未来基于芯粒的预填充-解码分离大语言模型服务系统的重要设计原则，3D堆叠架构为解决流量竞争问题提供了有效的技术路径。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) serving increasingly combines prefill-decode (PD) disaggregation with tensor parallelism (TP) to support large models and long contexts. In conventional 2D/2.5D chiplet architectures, layer-wise prefill-to-decode KV-cache transfer decode-side TP collectives share the same lateral die-to-die (D2D) interconnect, creating mixed-traffic contention on the decode critical path. This contention increases communication latency, prolongs token generation intervals, and degrades end-to-end serving performance. We propose 3DLS, a logic-on-logic 3D-stacked chiplet architecture that separates traffic classes by routing KV-cache transfers through vertical interconnects while preserving decode-side TP collectives on the lateral D2D fabric. 3DLS achieves up to 1.49$\times$ throughput and 60.2\% lower end-to-end (E2E) latency over the shared-fabric planar baseline, and still achieves up to 1.17$\times$ throughput and 31.4\% lower E2E latency over a workload-aware priority-managed planar baseline. These results highlight that physical isolation is an important design principle for future chiplet-based PD-disaggregated LLM serving systems.

</details>


### [192] [Approximate Attention Weighting for Sustainable FPGA-Based Vision Transformer Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01798)
*Muhammad Usman,Shujaat Khan,Dorit Merhof*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个无BRAM的近似注意力权重计算单元，通过16段分段线性函数近似自然指数，实现了资源高效的FPGA ViT推理，准确率损失仅0.20%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Vision Transformers在边缘视觉检测领域应用前景广阔，但在小型FPGA上部署面临挑战。自注意力机制中的softmax阶段需要指数运算和归一化，硬件开销大。现有实现依赖CORDIC流水线或BRAM查找表，导致面积和功耗增加。

Method: 提出一种无BRAM的近似注意力权重计算单元，使用16段分段线性函数完全通过分布式LUT逻辑实现自然指数近似。采用自然指数公式而非以2为底的近似，保留了预训练的注意力温度，避免了模型特定的重新校准。

Result: 在Xilinx Zynq-7020上实现的完整注意力行核心仅需1444个LUT和77个DSP，无需BRAM。硬件精确仿真显示，在ViT系列模型上，与精确softmax参考相比，Top-1准确率的绝对差异在0.20%以内。

Conclusion: 该设计证明了在资源受限的边缘AI平台上实现能效高的ViT推理的潜力，为边缘视觉检测应用提供了一种实用的硬件加速方案。

Abstract: Vision Transformers have reshaped computer vision by using self-attention to capture global context across image regions. This makes them attractive for edge visual inspection and monitoring in applications such as renewable-energy infrastructure, industrial quality control, medical imaging, and autonomous-system sensing. However, deploying ViTs on small FPGAs remains challenging because the softmax stage in self-attention requires exponential evaluation and normalization, which are costly in hardware. Existing implementations often rely on CORDIC pipelines or BRAM-based look-up tables, increasing area and power consumption. This paper presents a BRAM-free approximate attention-weighting unit for FPGA-based ViT inference. The proposed design approximates the natural exponential in softmax using a 16-segment piecewise-linear function implemented entirely with distributed LUT fabric. Unlike base-2 approximations, the natural-exponential formulation preserves the pre-trained attention temperature and avoids model-specific recalibration. Implemented on a Xilinx Zynq-7020, the complete attention-row core uses 1444 LUTs, 77 DSPs, and no BRAM, while hardware-accurate emulation shows accuracy within a \(0.20\%\) absolute top-1 difference from the exact-softmax reference on ViT-family models. These results demonstrate the potential of the proposed core for energy-efficient ViT inference on resource-constrained edge-AI platforms.

</details>


### [193] [Probabilistic Memory for Trustworthy Edge Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02465)
*Likai Pei,Jiahao Zheng,Xueji Zhao,Emilie Ye,Jianbo Liu,Hanqing Tao,Ming-Yen Lee,Ruiyang Qin,Yiyu Shi,Shimeng Yu,X. Sharon Hu,Ningyuan Cao*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出概率内存(p-MEM)统一原语，通过直接在内存带宽上采样实现超高吞吐量的高斯随机数生成，显著降低CPU/GPU系统在贝叶斯神经网络中的指令数、延迟和能耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 概率计算在可信边缘智能中至关重要，可用于量化不确定性、增强鲁棒性、重构数据和保护隐私。然而，高斯随机数生成(GRNG)与计算之间存在数量级的数据吞吐量差距，且存在指令开销问题，限制了概率计算的采用。

Method: 提出概率内存(p-MEM)统一内存原语，该原语存储分布参数(如均值和标准差)，并直接在原生内存带宽上进行采样，其中确定性数据成为零方差的特殊情况。使用经过布局验证的p-MEM模拟器，全面探索器件选择、内存规格和技术节点。

Result: p-MEM可实现超过1000 GSa/s/mm²的GRNG吞吐量（包含内存阵列访问）。集成到CPU/GPU系统后，在贝叶斯神经网络工作负载中，指令数减少高达2.19x/4.37x，采样延迟降低562x/3.45x，能耗降低295.5x/3.53x。

Conclusion: p-MEM为可信概率AI提供了可扩展的硬件基板，有效弥合了概率计算中的吞吐量差距，使概率计算能够在边缘智能中得到实际应用。

Abstract: Probabilistic computation plays an important role in trustworthy edge intelligence to quantify uncertainty, enhance robustness, reconstruct data, and protect privacy, but its adoption is limited by the orders-of-magnitude data throughput gap between Gaussian random number generation (GRNG) and computation, as well as instruction overhead. This paper introduces probabilistic memory (p-MEM), a unified memory primitive that stores distribution parameters, such as mean and standard deviation, and samples directly at the native memory bandwidth, where deterministic data becomes the zero-variance special case. Using a layout-validated p-MEM simulator, we comprehensively explore device choices, memory specifications, and technology nodes, showing that p-MEM can achieve more than 1000 GSa/s/mm^2 GRNG throughput, including memory-array access. Integrated into CPU/GPU systems, p-MEM reduces instruction count by up to 2.19x/4.37x, sampling latency by 562x/3.45x, and energy by 295.5x/3.53x for Bayesian neural network workloads, providing a scalable hardware substrate for trustworthy probabilistic AI.

</details>


<div id='cs.NI'></div>

# cs.NI [[Back]](#toc)

### [194] [Fully Unsupervised Detection of Physical Contacts on Subsea Cables via State-of-Polarization Monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01484)
*Agastya Raj,Alvaro Doval,Tian Tian,Steinar Bjørnstad,Marco Ruffini*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 提出了一种完全无监督的快速-慢速DSVDD检测器，用于部署的海底电缆的连续偏振态监测，无需事件标签训练即可有效识别电缆接触事件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在部署的海底电缆上进行连续的偏振态监测，以检测电缆接触事件（如拖网渔船接触），同时避免依赖昂贵且难以获取的事件标签数据。

Method: 开发了一种完全无监督的快速-慢速DSVDD（深度支持向量数据描述）检测器，该方法无需事件标签进行训练，能够对海底电缆的偏振态进行连续监测和异常检测。

Result: 在122,174条记录中，该方法将所有5个已确认的拖网渔船接触事件排在前13位，并发现了额外的经证实的电缆接触事件。

Conclusion: 该无监督检测方法在实际部署的海底电缆监测中表现出色，能够有效识别电缆接触事件，证明了无监督学习方法在实际工程应用中的价值和潜力。

Abstract: We present a fully unsupervised Fast-Slow DSVDD detector for continuous State-of-Polarization monitoring on a deployed subsea cable. Trained without event labels, it ranks all five confirmed trawler contacts within the top 13 of 122,174 recordings and surfaces additional corroborated cable-contact events.

</details>


### [195] [Enabling Real-Time AI in O-RAN: Deploying andMeasuring AI Inside a Near-RT RIC xApp](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01583)
*Lawrence Obiuwevwi,Krzysztof J. Rechowicz,Sampath Jayarathna,Safdar Hussain Bouk,Fahmida Afrin,C. Nicolas Barati,Neda Moghim,Valentina Nanou,Muhammad Enayetur Rahman,Sachin Shetty*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文在OAI和FlexRIC测试平台上实现了AI驱动的网络状态分类xApp，验证了轻量级AI模型可以嵌入Near-RT RIC软件循环中运行，推理延迟在微秒级，满足10毫秒的Near-RT时间预算要求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管AI在RAN优化领域已被广泛研究，但很少有工作在实际测试平台上演示AI推理直接嵌入Near-RT RIC软件循环中。O-RAN架构引入的可编程Near-RT RIC支持10毫秒到1秒时间尺度的闭环控制，需要验证AI能否在这些严格的时间约束下实际运行。

Method: 在OpenAirInterface (OAI) 和FlexRIC测试平台上实现AI驱动的网络状态分类xApp。使用结构化合成数据集训练模型，该数据集模拟跨层RAN状态，包含MAC、RLC、PDCP、GTP和UE计数特征。采用逻辑回归和浅层多层感知机(MLP)作为模型，将其导出为确定性C推理模块并编译到xApp二进制文件中，消除外部机器学习运行时依赖。

Result: 测量显示逻辑回归推理延迟为1-5微秒，MLP为10-25微秒，端到端服务延迟保持在4毫秒以下。六种模型对比显示监督模型准确率在0.88-0.90之间。噪声消融、混淆矩阵分析和基于CDF的延迟特征分析表明，两个嵌入模型在超过95%的预计循环执行中满足10毫秒Near-RT预算。

Conclusion: 轻量级AI模型可以在Near-RT RIC时间约束内运行，同时保持确定性执行。研究发布了RIC Workbench工具，这是一个轻量级编排仪表板，用于在商用硬件上复现测试平台环境。

Abstract: Open Radio Access Network (O-RAN) architectures introduce programmable Near-Real-Time RAN Intelligent Controllers (Near-RT RICs) that support closed-loop control through xApps at timescales from 10 ms to 1 s. Although AI has been widely studied for RAN optimization, fewer works demonstrate measured AI inference embedded directly within the Near-RT RIC software loop on a live testbed. This paper presents an AI-enabled network-state classification xApp implemented on an OpenAirInterface (OAI) and FlexRIC testbed. The xApp is trained and evaluated on a structured synthetic dataset that emulates cross-layer RAN states using MAC, RLC, PDCP, GTP, and UE-count features. The results validate embedding and execution feasibility rather than production-level generalization. Logistic regression and a shallow multilayer perceptron (MLP) are exported as deterministic C inference modules and compiled into the xApp binary, eliminating external machine-learning runtime dependencies. Measured inference latency is 1 to 5 microseconds for logistic regression and 10 to 25 microseconds for the MLP, while end-to-end service latency remains below 4 ms. A six-model comparison shows that supervised models achieve similar accuracy, ranging from 0.88 to 0.90, indicating that LR and MLP similarity reflects the proxy problem structure rather than limited model exploration. Noise ablation, confusion-matrix analysis, and CDF-based latency characterization show that both embedded models satisfy the 10 ms Near-RT budget for more than 95% of projected loop executions. These results demonstrate that lightweight AI can operate within Near-RT RIC timing constraints while preserving deterministic execution. We also release RIC Workbench, a lightweight orchestration dashboard for reproducing the testbed on commodity hardware.

</details>


### [196] [CSI Simulation: Why Additive Noise Fails and How to Fix It](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01882)
*Aymen Bouferroum,Ildi Alla,Vincent Lenders,Valeria Loscri*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 该论文挑战了CSI仿真中常用的AWGN加噪假设，发现自动增益控制会导致信道估计的非线性变换，提出M_QTC模型通过分位数映射等方法显著提升仿真数据保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CSI感知应用依赖仿真数据进行训练，通常假设接收链路线性且增益不变，使用AWGN加噪生成仿真数据。然而，每项目标条件下收集标注数据不切实际，该假设的有效性缺乏实证检验。研究者旨在验证这一假设是否成立，并解决仿真数据与真实数据之间的保真度差距问题。

Method: 使用射频干扰作为受控扰动，在2个室内环境的6个商用接收器上对假设进行实证测试。基于发现的问题，提出M_QTC测量校准模型，通过三个步骤学习每个子载波的分布变换：(1)分位数映射；(2)时间滤波；(3)基于Copula的跨子载波重排序。

Result: 实验证明传统假设不成立：自动增益控制在数字化前对信道估计进行乘性压缩，产生任何加性噪声方差都无法复现的幅度分布。M_QTC将幅度误差降低8倍，在四个维度上弥合了89%的综合保真度差距。在下游任务中，基于M_QTC仿真数据训练的5个不同类别分类器恢复了93%的真实数据干扰检测性能，而AWGN训练的分类器性能接近随机决策。

Conclusion: 传统CSI仿真中使用AWGN的基本假设不成立，自动增益控制的非线性效应不可忽视。M_QTC模型成功弥合了仿真数据与真实数据间的保真度差距，显著提升下游任务性能，为CSI仿真数据生成提供了更可靠的方法。

Abstract: Channel State Information (CSI) has become a widely used wireless channel sensing modality for applications such as indoor localization, activity recognition, and respiration monitoring. Because collecting labeled data under every target condition is impractical, training CSI-based models often relies on simulated data produced by adding noise or perturbations to recorded channel estimates, most commonly additive white Gaussian noise (AWGN). This practice assumes that the receiver chain between the antenna and the channel estimator is linear and gain-invariant. We test this assumption empirically using RF jamming as a controlled perturbation on 6 commodity receivers across 2 indoor environments. The assumption does not hold. Automatic gain control compresses the channel estimate multiplicatively before digitization, producing amplitude distributions that no additive noise variance can reproduce. To close the resulting fidelity gap, we propose M_QTC, a measurement-calibrated model that learns the per-subcarrier distribution transformation through quantile mapping, temporal filtering, and copula-based cross-subcarrier reordering. M_QTC reduces amplitude error 8-fold and closes 89% of the aggregate fidelity gap across four complementary dimensions. The improvement transfers directly to downstream tasks, where 5 classifiers from different families trained on M_QTC-simulated data recover 93% of real-data jamming detection performance, while AWGN-trained classifiers remain near random decision.

</details>


<div id='cs.DC'></div>

# cs.DC [[Back]](#toc)

### [197] [HYPIC: Accelerating Hybrid-Attention LLM Serving with Position-Independent Caching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01299)
*Yifei Liu,Juntong Wu,Yang Liu,Junhao Hu,Minghao Li,Xiaoxu Chen,Weihang Chen*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出了Hypic系统，首次实现了混合注意力LLM的位置无关缓存，通过段累积转移算子和边界接缝重计算策略，将TTFT降低2.45倍，峰值吞吐量提升至2.0倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在RAG和代理LLM服务中，提示词由独立片段组装成长上下文，导致预填充阶段主导单请求计算成本。现有的两种优化方向——位置无关缓存(PIC)和混合注意力模型无法共存：PIC无法应用于混合注意力模型，因为每token的KV缓存重用原语不适用于每请求的循环状态。

Method: 针对线性注意力层，识别出段累积转移算子作为缺失的代数原语，将其与每个段的零起始终端状态一起缓存，实现独立缓存段的近似精确且常数时间状态组合。对于全注意力层，发现最显著的注意力偏差集中在段边界处，因此仅需在每个边界重计算小的接缝窗口即可恢复跨段回看。此外，利用段级自包含性并行化缓存未命中的预填充。

Result: 在四个混合注意力模型和五个工作负载上的评估显示，Hypic将首token时间(TTFT)平均降低2.45倍，峰值吞吐量提升至多2.0倍，同时保持在完全重计算精度的3.3个百分点以内。

Conclusion: Hypic成功解决了混合注意力LLM与位置无关缓存的不兼容问题，为RAG和代理LLM服务提供了高效的推理加速方案，显著降低了长冷请求的尾延迟，填补了现有服务系统的技术空白。

Abstract: In retrieval augmented generation (RAG) and agentic LLM serving, prompts are assembled from independent segments into long contexts, making the prefill stage dominate the per-request computation cost. To this cost, two directions have emerged in parallel: position-independent caching (PIC) admits KV reuse for non-contiguous segments shared across different requests, while hybrid-attention models reduce computation complexity by replacing most full-attention layers with linear attention. However, they cannot coexist: applying PIC to hybrid-attention models breaks down because per-token KV-cache reuse primitives do not transfer to the per-request recurrent state.
  In this work, we present Hypic, the first serving system for hybrid-attention LLMs with position-independent caching. For linear-attention layers, we identify the segment-cumulative transition operator as the missing algebraic primitive, and cache it alongside each segment's zero-start end-state, enabling near-exact and constant-time state composition of independently cached segments. For the remaining full-attention layers, existing PIC methods also fail as linear layers do not expose the per-token hidden states for selective recomputation. We show that the most significant attention deviation concentrates at segment boundaries, so recomputing only a small seam window at each boundary suffices to restore cross-segment lookback. Finally, Hypic exploits segment-level self-containment to parallelize cache-miss prefill across instances, turning long cold requests -- a major tail-latency contributor under both prefix caching and prior PIC -- into an accelerable workload. Evaluated across four hybrid-attention models and five workloads, Hypic reduces time-to-first-token (TTFT) by 2.45x on average and improves peak throughput by up to 2.0x over existing systems, while staying within 3.3 points of full-recompute accuracy.

</details>


### [198] [SLFS: a Flexible, Low-Cost Distributed File System Using Serverless Designs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01486)
*Cheng Hao,Yang,Paola Alsharabaty,Soufiane Jounaid,Cristina Nita-Rotaru,Ji-Yong Shin*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出了SLFS，首个基于无服务器架构的分布式文件系统，通过毫秒级弹性伸缩和按需付费模式，以更低成本实现了优于现有系统的性能，并大幅缓解了冷启动问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大规模分布式文件系统资源配置针对峰值需求，但访问模式波动大导致非高峰期资源闲置；现有扩展机制粒度粗（服务器级别）、响应慢（分钟到小时级），无法应对文件系统常见的快速细粒度负载变化。无服务器计算的毫秒级弹性与按需计费提供了理想解决方案。

Method: 构建SLFS分布式文件系统：将数据和元数据操作均构建于无服务器函数之上；在键值存储之上实现文件服务，保持函数操作简单短小；引入新型多线程短生命周期服务器设计以克服冷启动问题同时维持低成本；设计策略执行协调器高效映射文件到函数实例、弹性扩展系统、控制函数生命周期以平衡性能与成本；支持S3等云原生服务或用户管理键值存储等多种存储后端，实现可配置的成本-性能权衡。

Result: SLFS相比基础无服务器设计将冷启动缓解580倍；与λFS、EFS和Ceph相比，成本分别降低高达63%、68%和63%。

Conclusion: SLFS证明了基于无服务器架构构建分布式文件系统的可行性，通过毫秒级弹性、按需计费和多种技术优化，实现了显著的成本节约和性能提升，为存储系统设计提供了新范式。

Abstract: Large-scale distributed file systems must provision resources for peak demand, yet file access patterns fluctuate significantly, leaving substantial capacity idle during off-peak periods. Existing scaling mechanisms operate at the granularity of entire servers and take minutes to hours, making them unable to track the rapid, fine-grained load variations that file systems commonly experience. Serverless computing, with its millisecond-granularity elasticity and pay-per-use pricing, offers a compelling alternative. We present SLFS, the first distributed file system built with serverless functions for both data and metadata operations. SLFS implements file services on top of key-value stores, keeping function operations simple and short, and introduces a novel multi-threaded, short-lived server design that overcomes the cold-start problem while maintaining low cost. A policy-enforcing coordinator efficiently maps files to function instances, scales the system elastically, and controls function lifetimes to balance performance and cost. SLFS can flexibly run on diverse storage backends -- from cloud-native services like S3 to user-managed key-value stores -- enabling configurable cost-performance trade-offs. Our evaluation shows that SLFS mitigates cold starts by 580$\times$ compared to the base serverless design and outperforms $λ$FS, EFS, and Ceph at up to 63%, 68%, and 63% lower cost, respectively.

</details>


### [199] [OmniPilot: An Uncertainty-Aware LLM Inference Advisor for Heterogeneous GPU Clusters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01579)
*D. Balamurugan,Thomas W. Bush*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出了OmniPilot，一个用于异构GPU集群上LLM服务配置的启动顾问系统，通过经济效用指标排名配置，在支持范围内实现95%的top-1准确率和6.2%的吞吐量预测MAPE，并能成功识别分布外场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在共享的异构GPU集群上部署大语言模型时，用户和运营商需要在提交宝贵节点时间之前选择GPU类型、张量并行度和精度，但这些选择面临多重挑战：有效吞吐量、启动成功率和集群需求利用率持续波动；静态配置方案忽略了量化效果依赖模型族、键值缓存压力产生尺寸-精度权衡、不同张量并行度的故障率差异超过两倍等关键交互作用；集群资源经常受到不可预测的硬件故障限制。

Method: OmniPilot采用共形校准的分位数成本模型（涵盖八个服务目标），结合分布外(OOD)弃权层。系统使用校准到运营商显示偏好的经济效用指标对配置进行排名。该启动顾问能够预测可行配置的服务成本，并在请求超出其测量的支持范围时选择弃权。

Result: 在A100、H100和H200硬件上跨四种精度的460次基准测试评估中，OmniPilot预测聚合吞吐量的平均绝对百分比误差(MAPE)为6.2%，对数空间R²=0.92。顾问系统实现95%的top-1准确率，平均效用遗憾仅为0.003。在分布外保留集测试中，预测误差上升至24-46%，共形区间覆盖5个点中的0个，但弃权层成功将所有五个识别为低置信度。

Conclusion: OmniPilot有效解决了异构GPU集群上LLM服务配置选择的挑战，在支持范围内实现了高精度的预测和配置推荐。系统的分布外检测机制能够可靠地识别低置信度预测，这些场景将被整合到训练数据集中以持续扩展顾问系统的支持范围，实现自适应改进。

Abstract: Serving large language models (LLMs) on a shared, heterogeneous GPU cluster requires users and operators to select the GPU type, tensor-parallel degree, and precision before committing valuable node-hours. Making these choices is challenging because effective throughput, launch-success rates, and cluster demand and utilization continuously fluctuate. Furthermore, static configuration recipes miss critical interactions: quantization effects depend heavily on the model family, key-value cache pressure creates size-by-precision trade-offs, and failure rates vary by more than twofold across different tensor-parallel degrees. Additionally, cluster resources are frequently constrained by unpredictable hardware failures. To address these challenges, we present \textbf{OmniPilot}, a launch advisor that predicts serving costs for feasible configurations and abstains when requests fall outside its measured support envelope. OmniPilot pairs a conformally calibrated quantile cost model (spanning eight serving targets) with an out-of-distribution (OOD) abstention layer. It ranks configurations using an economic utility metric calibrated to an operator's revealed preferences. In evaluations across 460 benchmark runs on A100, H100, and H200 hardware across four precisions, OmniPilot predicts aggregate throughput with a 6.2\% mean absolute percentage error (MAPE) and a log-space $R^2=0.92$. The advisor achieves 95\% top-1 accuracy with a mean utility regret of just 0.003. When tested on an OOD holdout of unsupported cells, prediction error climbs to 24-46\% and conformal intervals cover 0 of 5 points; however, the abstention layer successfully flags all five as low-confidence. Over time, these OOD scenarios will be integrated into the training dataset to continuously expand the advisor's support envelope.

</details>


### [200] [Securing People and their Machines Against Major Faults](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02304)
*Ohad Eitan,Idit Keidar,Ehud Shapiro*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向草根平台的容灾恢复方案，通过社交图谱、身份监护者和状态监护者的组合机制，实现了对私钥丢失和设备故障等重大故障的安全恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 草根平台是由自选公钥标识的人和其智能设备组成的分布式系统，缺乏全局资源支持。当用户遭遇私钥丢失或设备损坏等重大故障时，现有系统无法提供有效的恢复机制，因此需要一种不依赖中心化资源的对等恢复方案。

Method: 提出基于三个核心组件的恢复机制：（1）草根社交图谱，用户建立和维护友谊关系；（2）身份监护者，由用户指定的信任实体；（3）状态监护者，平台特定的恢复节点。当用户遭遇身份丢失时，在身份监护者超多数同意的前提下，社交图谱中的朋友节点协作替换旧公钥并恢复社交关系。仅设备损坏而密钥未丢的情况仅需状态监护者协助。形式化规范采用守护式多智能体原子事务，通过通信意愿智能体实现最终同步消息传递模型。

Result: 实现了安全社交图谱的具体方案，并证明了该实现能够将可恢复故障的执行映射到规范层的正确执行。对于草根货币和债券系统，展示了状态恢复的平台特定机制：货币的单写入者日志能够精确恢复，恢复后的主权实体可继续运行且无双花问题。

Conclusion: 该方案成功解决了草根平台在无全局资源依赖下的容灾恢复问题，证明了通过对等协作机制（社交图谱、监护者制度）可以有效应对身份丢失和设备故障。方法具有良好的通用性，可应用于社交图谱、数字货币和债券等多种草根平台场景。

Abstract: We consider grassroots platforms -- distributed systems of agents consisting of people identified by self-chosen public keys and their machines (smartphones) -- and wish to make them secure against \emph{major faults}: the loss of their private keys and/or their smartphones. As grassroots platforms have no global resource to rely on for recovery, our peer-based solution is based on: (\ia) \emph{a grassroots social graph} in which agents establish and maintain friendships; (\ib) \emph{identity custodians}, designated by each person, and (\ic) \emph{state custodians}, which are grassroots platform-specific. Upon a person experiencing identity loss, and given a willing supermajority of the identity custodians of the person, the friends of the person replace the old public key with the new one across the graph and restore friendships, where all friends serve as state custodians for the social graph. Choosing a new keypair, obtaining a new smartphone, and convincing identity custodians to will a change of key all happen ``off-chain''. Recovery from machine loss without loss of key (e.g. smartphone run over by truck, or its memory wiped) is simpler, requiring only the help of state custodians.
  We specify the social graph and its secure version as guarded multiagent atomic transactions, and implement the secure social graph via communicating volitional agents, an eventually synchronous message-passing model one step closer to implementation. We prove the implementation maps runs with recoverable faults to correct runs of the specification.
  We follow a similar path for grassroots coins and bonds, showing a common core as well as the platform-specific aspects of state recovery: a currency's single-writer log is recovered exactly, the recovered sovereign resuming without double-spending.

</details>


### [201] [Arachne: Orchestrating Cascades for Efficient Text-to-Video Model Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01701)
*Peng Yu,Yuankai Fan,Yang Qiu,Tian Li,Bihuan Chen,Yin Chen,Qizhen Weng*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出了Arachne框架，通过细粒度的级联计算单元和协调优化策略，解决了T2V模型训练中的负载不均衡问题，将迭代时间减少高达65%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有T2V模型训练采用分桶策略处理异构视频数据，但在静态并行方案下难以随计算和数据规模扩展，导致严重的负载不均衡和硬件利用率低下。

Method: 提出Arachne框架，将训练过程分解为细粒度的计算单元（cascades），通过协调的空间和时间优化策略，在集群中编排这些单元的分布式执行和同步。

Result: 实验表明，Arachne相比主流框架将迭代时间减少高达65%，且随着训练规模增长，性能优势呈现正向扩展趋势。

Conclusion: Arachne有效解决了大规模T2V模型训练的可扩展性问题，实现了显著的性能提升，且优势随规模增大而增强。

Abstract: The rising demand for AI-generated videos is fueled by advances in large-scale Text-to-Video (T2V) models, trained on extensive datasets of video clips spanning diverse resolutions and durations. To address this data heterogeneity, current training methods often use a bucketing strategy that groups samples into discrete buckets for efficiency. However, this approach struggles to scale with compute and data volumes under static parallelism schemes, such as data and sequence parallelism, leading to significant workload imbalances and hardware under-utilization.
  In this paper, we present Arachne, a novel training framework for efficient T2V model training at scale. Arachne decomposes the training process into fine-grained computational units, called \textit{cascades}, orchestrating their distributed execution and synchronization across the cluster through coordinated spatial and temporal optimization. Our comprehensive evaluation demonstrates that Arachne reduces iteration time by up to 65\% over leading frameworks, exhibiting a positive scaling trend where its performance advantages amplify as training scale grows.

</details>


### [202] [Exploiting Task-Based Parallelism for the Red-Black Gauss-Seidel Method on 2D Grids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01735)
*Shiting Long,Gustavo Ramirez-Hidalgo,Andreas Frommer,Dirk Pleiter*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文实现了基于任务编程模型的红黑高斯-赛德尔方法，相比传统分治并行方法，在保持相当性能的同时提供了更好的硬件级异步性容忍度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统分治并行策略实现多色高斯-赛德尔方法存在全局同步开销和负载不平衡问题，限制了并行效率。任务编程模型通过细粒度并行、消除全局屏障和允许不同颜色更新在时间上部分重叠，有望缓解这些问题。

Method: 使用两种任务编程模型实现红黑高斯-赛德尔方法，并与经典分治并行实现进行对比。以二维泊松方程求解为基准测试，评估细粒度并行对执行效率的影响。

Result: 基于任务的实现在性能上可与传统的分治并行化方法相当，同时对硬件级异步性具有更强的适应性和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 任务编程模型是解决传统并行迭代求解方法同步和负载平衡问题的有效途径，红黑方案作为代表案例，其方法可自然扩展到非结构网格和更宽模板产生的通用多色方案。

Abstract: Gauss-Seidel is a well-established iterative method for the solution of linear systems, and multicoloring has been widely used to increase parallelism in iterative solution techniques. Implementing multi-color Gauss-Seidel with conventional divide-and-conquer parallelization strategies, however, may be inefficient due to global synchronization requirements and load imbalances. Task-based programming models can mitigate these issues by enabling fine-grained parallelism, removing global barriers and allowing updates of different colors to partially overlap in time. In this work, we implement the red-black Gauss-Seidel method using two task-based programming models and compare them with a classical divide-and-conquer parallel implementation to evaluate the impact of fine-grained parallelism on execution efficiency. The red-black scheme serves as a representative example, as task-based approaches naturally extend to more general multi-color schemes arising from unstructured grids and wider stencils. Using the solve of the 2D Poisson equation as benchmark, our results show that task-based implementations can achieve performance comparable to conventional divide-and-conquer parallelization while providing greater resilience to hardware-level asynchronicity.

</details>


### [203] [HCMS: Head-Chunked Multi-Stream Pipeline for Communication-Computation Overlap in Long-Sequence Parallel Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01817)
*Chao Yuan,Pan Li,Yingnan Sun,Jing Liu*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出HCMS方法，通过多头注意力分块和双CUDA流实现细粒度通信-计算重叠，在视频生成等中长序列场景下相比Ulysses和Ring Attention加速10%-17.5%和5%-14.5%，无需修改底层算子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有全连接序列并行方法在处理中长序列时通信与计算严格串行执行，导致硬件资源利用率不足。

Method: Head-Chunked Multi-Stream Pipeline (HCMS)利用多头注意力的计算独立性，将注意力头划分为多个块，通过双CUDA流实现细粒度的通信-计算重叠；与FlashAttention和SDPA等现有优化正交兼容，无需修改底层kernel，支持非均匀划分且保持数值等价。

Result: 在4个GPU平台的2-8 GPU规模实验中，对于31K-56K token的视频生成序列长度，HCMS相比Ulysses基线加速10%-17.5%，相比Ring Attention加速5%-14.5%；在Wan2.2模型上实现6.8%端到端加速。理论分析表明HCMS收益与通信比例ρ正相关，建议在ρ>20%时使用。

Conclusion: HCMS通过通信计算重叠有效提升中长序列并行效率，且具有良好的兼容性和实用性，为视频生成等长序列场景提供了有效的优化方案。

Abstract: All-to-all based sequence parallelism methods execute communication and computation strictly in serial when processing medium-long sequences, resulting in hardware resource underutilization. This paper proposes Head-Chunked Multi-Stream Pipeline (HCMS), which exploits the computational independence of multi-head attention by partitioning attention heads into multiple chunks and achieving fine-grained communication-computation overlap through dual CUDA streams. HCMS is orthogonally compatible with existing optimizations such as FlashAttention and SDPA, requires no modification to underlying kernels, supports uneven partitioning while maintaining numerical equivalence. Experiments validate the effectiveness across four GPU platforms at 2-8 GPU scales: for typical video generation sequence lengths of 31K-56K tokens, HCMS achieves 10\%-17.5\% speedup over the Ulysses baseline and 5\%-14.5\% speedup over Ring Attention; end-to-end acceleration of 6.8\% is achieved on the Wan2.2 model. Theoretical analysis shows that HCMS benefits are positively correlated with communication ratio $ρ$, and its use is recommended when $ρ>20\%$.

</details>


### [204] [Lynx: Progressive Speculative Quantization for accelerating KV Transfer in Long-Context Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01831)
*Wenchen Han,Gingfung Matthew Yeung,Marco Barletta,William Toner,Amory Hoste,Adam Barker*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: Lynx提出渐进式KV缓存传输方案，通过将KV缓存分为高优先级Anchor流和低优先级Residual流，实现了与激进4-bit量化相当的TTFT，同时保持BF16高精度推理的准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文推理在LLM服务中日益普遍，需要在 disaggregated 推理架构中通过网络传输大量KV缓存。现有KV量化技术虽然减少了数据量，但无法同时实现低网络延迟和高推理精度。传统方法将KV缓存视为必须完全接收才能使用的不可分割单元，导致解码必须等待传输完成。

Method: 挑战KV缓存必须完全接收才能使用的假设，利用KV缓存中不同bit对注意力计算贡献不平等的观察：最高有效位捕获注意力粗粒度结构，最低有效位细化精度。提出Lynx系统，将KV缓存分割为携带最高有效位的高优先级Anchor流和携带剩余精度的低优先级Residual流。解码在收到Anchor流后立即开始并进行推测执行，同时并发传输Residual流，最后通过验证确保与高精度解码等价。

Result: 在多个模型和服务负载上，Lynx实现了与激进4-bit KV量化相当的TTFT，同时匹配高精度（BF16）推理的准确性；相比标准8-bit KV量化将TTFT提升最多1.43倍，相比最先进方法将准确性提升最多5.1%。

Conclusion: Lynx通过渐进式、分流的KV传输机制成功解决了长上下文推理中的网络延迟与推理精度权衡问题，证明了KV缓存可以部分使用的可行性，为 disaggregated 推理架构中的KV缓存传输提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Long-context inference is increasingly common in large language model (LLM) serving, driven by retrieval-augmented generation and agentic systems. In disaggregated inference, these workloads require transferring large Key-Value (KV) caches across the network, where decoding cannot begin until the transfer completes. Recent KV quantization techniques reduce data volume and alleviate this bottleneck, but existing schemes fail to achieve both low network-exposed latency and high inference accuracy.
  We challenge the assumption that the KV cache is an indivisible unit that must be fully received before use. We leverage the observation that different bits in the KV cache contribute unequally to attention computation and inference precision: the most significant bits capture the coarse structure of attention and the least significant bits refine precision. This property enables partial use of the KV cache during decoding. We present Lynx, a system that enables progressive, split-stream KV transfer by partitioning the KV cache into a high-priority Anchor stream carrying the most significant bits and a low-priority Residual stream carrying remaining precision. Decoding begins upon receipt of the Anchor stream and proceeds speculatively while the Residual stream is transferred concurrently, followed by verification that ensures equivalence to higher-precision decoding.
  Across multiple models and serving workloads, Lynx achieves Time-to-First-Token (TTFT) comparable to aggressive 4-bit KV quantization, while matching the accuracy of high-precision (BF16) inference, improving TTFT over standard 8-bit KV quantization by up to $1.43\times$ and improving accuracy over state-of-the-art by up to $5.1\%$.

</details>


### [205] [Mixture-of-Parallelisms: Towards Memory-Efficient Training Stack for Mixture-of-Experts Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01844)
*Xuan-Phi Nguyen,Shrey Pandit,Yiran Zhao,Semih Yavuz,Silvio Savarese,Shafiq Joty*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向MoE模型的内存高效训练框架MoP，通过组合现有与新颖的并行策略，在不到12个8x H200节点上实现万亿参数规模与百万上下文长度的无损预训练/微调，相比强优化的FSDP2基线取得4.7–8.2倍的单GPU吞吐提升，并将可训练上下文长度扩展至100万tokens。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MoE模型训练在内存与通信带宽约束下难以高效扩展到万亿参数与百万级上下文长度；FSDP2等强基线在长上下文下易OOM且吞吐有限。需在CPU/GPU内存、CPU-GPU/GPU-GPU/节点间带宽等物理约束下，最大化训练效率与内存利用率。

Method: 提出MoP训练范式：1）在MoE流水线的不同层与阶段，结合并特化多种现有与新颖的并行技术（如专家并行、张量并行等），按物理约束动态选择；2）引入新型优化器步策略以提升吞吐与内存效率；3）统筹CPU、CPU内存、GPU HBM与各类通信带宽，最大化资源利用与并行度。

Result: 在约12个8x H200节点上实现万亿参数规模与百万上下文长度的无损预训练/微调，达到SOTA吞吐与内存效率；相较强优化的FSDP2基线，MoP实现4.7x–8.2x更高的单GPU吞吐（规模越大优势越显著），并将可训练上下文长度扩展至1M tokens（基线在64–128K后OOM）。

Conclusion: MoP通过跨层与跨阶段的特化并行与新型优化器策略，在严格硬件约束下实现万亿参数、百万上下文的高效可扩展训练，显著超越强基线FSDP2的吞吐与内存能力，为超大MoE模型训练提供了实用高效的方案。

Abstract: This paper showcases a memory-efficient training stack for Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models. It is a training paradigm that combines and specializes various existing and novel parallelism techniques at different layers and stages of the Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) model training pipeline. It leverages these techniques to achieve maximal efficiency given the physical constraints of CPU, CPU memory, GPU HBM memory, and the CPU-GPU, GPU-GPU, and node-node communication bandwidth of the GPU cluster. It also contains a novel strategy for the optimizer step to achieve high throughput and memory efficiency, enabling practitioners to conduct lossless pre-training/fine-tuning of trillion-parameter scale models, at a million context length, with just under 12 8x H200 GPU nodes, with state-of-the-art throughput and memory efficiency. In our experiments, MoP delivers 4.7x--8.2x higher per-GPU throughput than a strongly-tuned FSDP2 baseline (with the gap widening at larger scale) and sustains training at context lengths up to 1M tokens, where the baseline runs out of memory beyond 64--128K.

</details>


### [206] [Towards Load-Aware Prefill Deflection for Disaggregated LLM Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02043)
*Shrikara Arun,Anjaly Parayil,Srikant Bharadwaj,Renee St. Amant,Victor Rühle*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种主动预填充偏转调度器，通过让解码节点以分块方式处理请求的预填充阶段，解决了分离式LLM服务中预填充节点饱和而解码节点计算资源利用率不足的问题，将P95 TTFT降低了高达81%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在分离式LLM服务中，预填充和解码阶段在不同GPU池上运行以避免相互干扰。然而在实际的突发、重尾工作负载下，会出现新的不对称性：预填充节点达到饱和，而解码节点的计算资源利用率不足。在生产环境的A100集群（2P2D配置）中，预填充执行仅占P95 TTFT的2-23%，其余时间消耗在排队和节点间GPU-GPU KV缓存传输上。

Method: 提出主动预填充偏转调度器：允许解码节点以分块预填充步骤的方式处理请求的预填充阶段，并与正在进行的解码批次交错执行。对于每个排队的请求，估计其在预填充节点上的TTFT，并在每个解码节点上搜索最大的分块调度方案，使正在进行的解码保持在TBT SLO范围内，当解码路径有助于降低尾延迟时进行偏转。由于偏转请求的预填充阶段在解码节点本地执行，消除了节点间KV传输。

Result: 在vLLM上实现，使用DeepSeek-V2-Lite在生产风格trace下评估：相比最先进的分离式调度器，P95 TTFT降低高达81%，SLO达成率提升高达79%，每个请求的路由开销低于1毫秒。

Conclusion: 该研究成功解决了分离式LLM服务中预填充节点饱和与解码节点资源闲置的不对称问题，通过创新的预填充偏转调度策略，显著降低了尾延迟并提高了服务等级目标达成率，为生产环境中的LLM服务优化提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Disaggregated LLM serving runs prefill and decode on separate GPU pools to keep the two phases from interfering. In practice, this creates a new asymmetry: under bursty, heavy-tailed workloads prefill nodes saturate while decode nodes have compute underutilized, and on a production-style A100 cluster with 2 prefill and 2 decode nodes (2P2D), we find that prefill execution accounts for only 2-23% of P95 Time-to-First-Token (TTFT). Queuing and inter-node GPU-GPU KV-cache transfer account for the rest. We present a proactive prefill-deflecting scheduler that lets decode nodes serve prefill phase of requests as chunked-prefill steps interleaved with their in-flight decode batches. For each queued request, we estimate the TTFT it would see on the prefill node, and on every decode node, search for the largest chunk schedule that keeps in-flight decodes within their Time-Between-Tokens (TBT) SLO and deflect when the decode path helps tail latency. Because the prefill phase of deflected requests runs in place on the decode node, the inter-node KV transfer is eliminated. Implemented on vLLM and evaluated on production-style traces with DeepSeek-V2-Lite, our approach reduces P95 TTFT by upto 81% and raises SLO attainment by upto 79% over state-of-the-art disaggregated schedulers, at sub-millisecond per-request routing cost.

</details>


### [207] [Cadence: Extreme Pipelining with Multiple Concurrent Proposers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02275)
*Fatima Elsheimy,Mohammad Mussadiq Jalalzai,Tobias Klenze,Jovan Komatovic,Mike Setrin,Victor Shoup,Kushal Babel,Lioba Heimbach,Jason Milionis*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: Cadence是首个具有任意低出块间隔、最优韧性和最优快速路径延迟的拜占庭容错多提议者共识协议，在快速路径延迟下实现了短期抗审查和隐藏性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统单领导者共识存在领导者垄断交易排序的问题，可能导致审查；现有协议出块间隔受限于网络延迟；需要实现多提议者环境下的抗审查和低延迟共识。

Method: 提出"极端流水线"框架，将时间划分为等距时隙，每个时隙一个区块，各共识实例独立运行互不等待；将任意满足slot-consensus规范的协议转换为多次协议；设计Chorus协议实现三轮快速路径最终化；设计Conductor协调器以稳定节奏开启时隙。

Result: 在异步条件下具备最优韧性(n=3f+1)，证明了安全性、活性、抗审查性和隐藏性；在Monad 200个验证者、每时隙5个提议者的模拟中，最终化平均219ms（推测最终性167ms）；100ms出块间隔下交易平均等待50ms进入提案。

Conclusion: Cadence成功解耦了出块间隔与网络延迟，在多提议者共识中实现了单领导者共识的最优快速路径延迟，同时保证了短期抗审查和隐藏性，为高性能区块链系统提供了新的共识范式。

Abstract: We present Cadence, a Byzantine fault-tolerant multi-proposer consensus protocol with arbitrarily low block intervals, optimal resilience, and optimal fast-path latency. Cadence divides time into equally spaced slots, one block per slot, each finalized in its own consensus instance. Blocks do not build directly on their predecessor, so instances run independently and none waits for an earlier block to finish or propagate; we call this extreme pipelining, decoupling the block interval from network latency. Cadence also removes the single-leader monopoly over transaction inclusion and ordering: under multiple concurrent proposers (MCP), several validators propose for each block, and it guarantees that, under synchrony, a transaction a correct proposer includes cannot be censored or deferred (short-term censorship resistance), and that no proposer can craft its proposal in reaction to the others' (hiding). To realize extreme pipelining, we introduce a general framework that turns any one-shot consensus meeting our slot-consensus specification into a multi-shot protocol. We instantiate it for MCP with two protocols of our own: Chorus, a slot consensus whose fast path finalizes a block in an optimal three rounds, with speculative finality one round earlier, and Conductor, an orchestrator that opens slots at an even cadence, more slowly under asynchrony to keep open slots bounded. To our knowledge, Cadence is the first MCP protocol to provide short-term censorship resistance and hiding at the fast-path latency of single-leader consensus. We prove safety, liveness, censorship resistance, and hiding under partial synchrony with optimal resilience (n = 3f+1). In simulation over Monad's 200 validators with five proposers per slot, finalization averages 219 ms (167 ms to speculative finality); at a 100 ms block interval a transaction waits on average 50 ms to enter a proposal.

</details>


### [208] [Elasticity in Parallel Sparse Triangular Solve](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02324)
*Raphael S. Steiner,Christos K. Matzoros,Pál András Papp,Toni Böhnlein,A. N. Yzelman*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了陈旧同步并行(SSP)执行模式和ElasticDivide调度器，用于并行稀疏三角线性系统求解，在48核ARM和x86机器上分别实现7-30%和19-60%的加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的同步并行调度器在稀疏三角求解中存在同步开销问题，限制了并行性能。作者希望通过允许同步与计算重叠来减少同步开销，从而提高并行效率。

Method: 提出陈旧同步并行(SSP)执行模式，并设计了基于有向无环图(DAG)的通用调度器ElasticDivide，该调度器能够生成允许同步与计算重叠的调度方案。

Result: 在48核ARM机器上，相比先进的同步调度器GrowLocal获得7-30%的几何平均加速；在48核x86机器上，相比SpMP获得19-60%的几何平均加速。

Conclusion: 陈旧同步并行执行模式在并行稀疏三角求解中具有显著优势，能够有效重叠同步与计算，提升并行性能，ElasticDivide调度器在多核架构上表现出色。

Abstract: We introduce stale synchronous parallel as a mode of execution in parallel sparse triangular linear system solve and present a general directed-acyclic-graph scheduler capable of producing such schedules. Stale-synchronous-parallel schedules allow the overlap of synchronisation and compute which results in a geometric-mean speed-up of $7$-$30\%$ of our scheduler, ElasticDivide, over state-of-the-art synchronous scheduler GrowLocal on an ARM machine using 48 cores. On an x86 machine using 48 cores, we report geometric-mean speed-ups of $19$-$60\%$ over SpMP.

</details>


### [209] [WattGPU: Predicting Inference Power and Latency on Unseen GPUs and LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02391)
*Mauricio Fadel Argerich,Jonathan Fürst,Marta Patiño-Martínez*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出了WattGPU，一种无需硬件访问或性能分析即可预测LLM在GPU上的功耗和延迟的框架，在未见过的GPU和LLM上实现了比传统基线方法低2-4倍的预测误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM推理工作负载正迅速增长并成为数据中心能耗的主要贡献者。优化部署需要将特定LLM匹配到最高效的GPU，但运营商目前缺乏在不进行详尽性能分析的情况下实现这一目标的工具。现有预测模型仍需性能分析数据，且难以泛化到训练期间未见过的硬件。

Method: 提出了WattGPU，包含两个预测模型，分别用于预测平均GPU功耗和Token间延迟（ITL）。该方法仅利用公开可用的LLM元数据和GPU规格，无需硬件访问或性能分析，从而能够泛化到未见过的NVIDIA服务器级GPU和LLM。在包含42个开源LLM（0.1B-27B参数）和8个GPU的数据集上，使用留一GPU法和留一LLM法交叉验证进行评估。

Result: 在未见过的GPU上，平均功耗模型在离线和服务器场景下的中位绝对百分比误差分别≤3.4%和≤13.5%；延迟模型在服务器模式下误差≤8.5%，且服务器场景下GPU排名相关性保持较强（Kendall τ≥0.76）。与标准物理基线（功耗的负载缩放TDP和延迟的Roofline模型）相比，在服务器场景下的未见LLM-GPU组合上，误差降低约4倍，在完全未见过的GPU上降低约2倍。

Conclusion: WattGPU成功证明了仅利用公开元数据和规格即可实现高精度的LLM-GPU性能预测，显著优于传统方法，为数据中心运营商提供了一种无需硬件访问即可优化LLM部署的有效工具。数据和代码已公开。

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM) inference workloads are a rapidly growing contributor to data center energy consumption. Optimizing these deployments requires matching specific LLMs to the most efficient GPUs, but operators currently lack the tools to do so without exhaustively profiling each combination. While some predictive models exist, they still require profiling data and struggle to generalize to hardware unseen during training. To address this, we introduce \textit{WattGPU}, featuring two predictive models for mean GPU power draw and Inter-Token Latency (ITL). Our approach leverages only publicly available LLM metadata and GPU specifications, eliminating the need for hardware access or profiling while enabling generalization to unseen NVIDIA server-grade GPUs and LLMs. We evaluate our models using rigorous leave-one-GPU-out and leave-one-LLM-out cross-validation on a dataset of 42 open-source LLMs (0.1B--27B parameters) and 8 GPUs under both offline and server scenarios. The mean power draw model achieves a median absolute percentage error of $\leq3.4\%$ for offline and $\leq13.5\%$ for server scenarios on unseen GPUs, while the latency model achieves $\leq8.5\%$ in server mode, both maintaining strong GPU ranking correlations for server scenarios (Kendall $τ\geq0.76$). Compared to standard physically grounded baselines -- Load-Scaled Thermal Design Power (TDP) for power draw and roofline for latency -- our models reduce median absolute percentage error by approximately 4$\times$ on unseen LLM-GPU combinations for server scenarios or approximately 2$\times$ for completely unseen GPUs. WattGPU's data and code are publicly available at https://github.com/maufadel/wattgpu.

</details>


### [210] [FlintKV: A Fast Durable Storage Engine for Modern Databases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02401)
*Sergey Egorov,Gregory Chockler,Brijesh Dongol,Dan O'Keeffe,Sadegh Keshavarzi*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了FlintKV，一种基于跳表并针对NVM优化的存储引擎，原生支持生产级键值存储的完整API（如快照、一致迭代器、原子批量写入），通过新型flat-combining并发控制算法实现高性能与可扩展性，端到端吞吐量相比现有工作提升高达75%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有NVM键值存储虽然在写入和点查询上表现优异，但缺乏或未充分支持现代数据库所需的丰富接口保障（如时间点快照、一致迭代器、原子批量写入），而这些特性对于事务和并发控制至关重要。生产级键值引擎（如RocksDB）已提供这些功能，但在NVM环境下的高效实现仍是空白。

Method: 提出FlintKV，采用NVM优化的跳表结构，核心是一个新型的基于flat-combining的并发控制算法，结合多版本机制和精心协同设计的持久化机制，同时保证持久化线性一致性。支持原子批量写入和快照一致迭代，可独立部署或将其持久化跳表集成到现有NVM存储中以增强功能。

Result: 实验评估表明，FlintKV在端到端吞吐量上相比先前工作可提升高达75%。

Conclusion: FlintKV成功弥合了NVM键值存储与生产级功能需求之间的差距，证明了在NVM上高效支持完整键值存储API的可行性，为现代数据库的事务和并发控制提供了高性能基础设施。

Abstract: Byte-addressable non-volatile memory (NVM) offers an opportunity to rethink storage engine architectures. While recent NVM key-value stores achieve high throughput for ingestion and point lookups, they omit or under-specify the support for the richer interface guarantees required by modern databases. Production key-value engines (e.g., RocksDB) provide point-in-time snapshots, consistent iterators, and atomic batches-features essential for implementing transactions and concurrency control. We present FlintKV, an NVM-optimized skiplist-based storage engine that natively supports the full API of production key-value stores. FlintKV supports both atomic batch writes and snapshot-consistent iteration efficiently while guaranteeing durable linearizability. FlintKV can be deployed standalone or its durable skiplist can be integrated into existing NVM stores to enhance their capabilities. Central to FlintKV is a novel flat-combining based concurrency control algorithm that leverages multi-versioning and carefully co-designed persistence mechanisms to ensure high performance and scalability. Our empirical evaluation shows that FlintKV can achieve up to a 75% improvement in end-to-end throughput over prior work.

</details>


<div id='cs.MA'></div>

# cs.MA [[Back]](#toc)

### [211] [Cache Merging as a Convergent Replicated State for Multi-Agent Latent Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01308)
*Carlos Baquero,Luís Brito*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 提出CanonicalMerge方法解决多智能体潜在推理中的KV-cache合并问题，通过基于内容的排序实现了输入顺序无关且幂等的合并操作，在无需预知最优顺序的情况下达到最佳性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的BagMerge方法通过沿序列轴拼接缓存并重新编码RoPE来合并多智能体的KV-cache，但该操作具有非交换性：最优的输入顺序不可预测，且会随实验设置、潜在步数预算和模型规模而变化。这种不确定性导致系统行为不稳定，需要一种能提供结构化保证的合并方法。

Method: 提出CanonicalMerge方法，将合并操作转化为收敛的复制状态：(1) 通过中间层的平均K-norm对缓存进行内容排序，使合并结果在任意输入置换下字节相同；(2) 将复制状态与解码时布局分离：状态是内容寻址的潜在片段集合，其合并是集合并操作，构成状态型CvRDT（交换、结合、幂等、吸收）；(3) CanonicalMerge作为该状态的确定性渲染，实现幂等性——重复输入被吸收而非重复拼接。

Result: 在分区推理基准测试中，CanonicalMerge在每个设置-预算-排序组合中都匹配了最佳BagMerge排序的性能，且无需预知最优顺序，准确率损失在统计上不显著。在真实多文档QA任务(HotpotQA)上表现良好，而最接近的无训练输出融合基线(PackLLM)在相同预算下落后45个百分点。在k>2时方法能够迁移并共置潜在轨迹但尚未实现组合。

Conclusion: CanonicalMerge成功将KV-cache级别的合并操作从顺序依赖的非确定性问题转化为具有结构化保证的确定性问题，证明了缓存级合并与输出级融合属于不同范式。该方法为多智能体潜在推理提供了稳定的基础，同时指出k>2时的组合问题是未来研究的重要方向。

Abstract: Multi-agent latent reasoning composes agents' KV-caches into one context for a final agent. Prior work (Agent Primitives) does this by concatenating caches along the sequence axis with RoPE re-encoding, which we call BagMerge. BagMerge is non-commutative, and the best input ordering is unpredictable, shifting with the regime, the latent-step budget, and the model scale. We make this exchange a convergent replicated state. First, CanonicalMerge fixes the layout by content: ordering caches by mean K-norm at a middle layer renders the merged cache byte-identical under any input permutation, verified algorithmically (arity N<=5) and bit-for-bit on real Qwen3-1.7B and 4B state. Second, we separate the replicated state from decode-time layout: the state is a set of content-addressed latent fragments whose merge is set union, a state-based CvRDT (commutative, associative, idempotent, absorbing), and CanonicalMerge is its deterministic render. Because the render is byte-equivalent, every N=2 accuracy number carries over unchanged and re-delivered duplicates are absorbed rather than re-concatenated. On a partitioned-reasoning benchmark, CanonicalMerge matches the best BagMerge ordering in every regime-by-budget-by-ordering cell without knowing which order is best, trading a small, statistically insignificant accuracy margin for an unconditional structural guarantee. The behaviour transfers to real multi-document QA (HotpotQA), while the closest training-free output-fusion baseline (PackLLM) loses by 45 points at matched budget, placing cache-level merging in a regime distinct from output-level fusion. Finally, at k>2 the approach transports and colocates latent traces but does not by itself compose them, which we characterize to motivate future work.

</details>


### [212] [Simulation Based Reward Function Validation for Multi-Agent On Orbit Inspection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01367)
*Patrick Quinn,Bala Prenith Reddy Gopu,George M. Nehma,Madhur Tiwari*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于3D重建分析的通用奖励函数，用于多智能体强化学习（MARL）控制航天器群进行在轨检测，使智能体能够自主决定图像采集时机。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MARL在轨检测任务的奖励函数仅关注到达预定的有限检测点，缺乏灵活性。本研究旨在开发一种通用奖励函数，允许智能体在任意位置评估图像并自主控制采集时机。

Method: 通过对在轨检测物体的3D重建分析，设计了一种通用奖励函数，使其能够评估任意位置和任意数量的图像，并赋予训练智能体对图像采集时机的完全控制权。

Result: 该方法的奖励函数具有通用性，可评估任意数量的图像；智能体能够自主决定图像采集时机；获得了MARL检测任务的最佳实践以及在更广泛检测任务中的关键见解。

Conclusion: 提出的通用奖励函数不仅适用于特定MARL检测任务，还为更广泛背景下的检测任务提供了重要参考和实践经验。

Abstract: A proposed method for the control of groups of inspection spacecraft is Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning (MARL). While MARL has already been employed for this purpose in previous work, the reward functions used focus on reaching a finite set of predetermined inspection points around the target. In this work, we study and develop a generalized reward function for the MARL inspection task informed by the analysis of 3D reconstructions of inspected objects in orbit. Because the reward function is generalized such that any number of images at arbitrary locations may evaluated, we also allow trained agents to have complete control over when images are collected. With this approach, we gather insights into best practices for not only the specific MARL inspection task, but also gain key takeaways informative to the broader inspection task outside of a MARL context.

</details>


### [213] [Congestion-Based Slot Pricing in a Railway Auction Game](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01822)
*Bill Roungas,Sebastiaan Meijer*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文研究了一个用于分配离散拥堵资源的多智能体系统，结合拥堵定价和非对称矫正机制来缓解大型代理商的战略优势，在铁路时刻分配场景中验证了该方法的有效性和局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机源于铁路行业放松管制后的时刻分配问题。在多智能体环境中，不同规模和能力的运营商代理商需要竞争有限的拥堵资源，大型代理商可能通过战略性行为获得不公平优势，需要设计一种既能缓解战略优势又能保持透明度和拥堵敏感性的分配机制。

Method: 设计了一个实时、基于网络的多智能体实验环境，人类参与者控制个体代理商进行重复拍卖博弈。机制包含两个关键组成部分：(1)基于拥堵的基础价格，随总需求增加而上升；(2)非对称矫正调整机制，惩罚请求最多时刻的代理商，奖励请求最少的代理商。博弈建模为不完全信息下的重复博弈，参与者可获得边际成本和竞争对手反馈信息。

Result: 通过两场由领域专家参与的实验发现：拥堵机制按设计响应总需求，矫正激励机制被成功激活；但代表大型运营商的代理商尽管面临惩罚仍坚持高请求策略，表明矫正定价必要但不足以消除多智能体环境中的战略优势。事后复盘显示参与者的决策由所扮演的代理商角色而非个人特质驱动，除了短期利润最大化外还存在保持市场存在感和提高竞争对手成本等战略动机。

Conclusion: 研究表明在非对称预算条件下，仅依靠矫正定价机制不足以完全抑制大型代理商的战略优势，需要进一步探索更有效的多智能体机制设计方案。为未来研究指明了两个方向：分析性验证和大规模多智能体实验，以更好地理解代理商行为和优化机制设计。

Abstract: We present a multi-agent system for studying the allocation of discrete, congested resources among heterogeneous strategic agents, motivated by the problem of railway slot allocation under deregulation. Multiple operator-agents, differing in size and capacity, interact through a shared auction mechanism over repeated rounds under time-constrained decision-making. The mechanism combines a congestion-based base price that increases with aggregate demand with an asymmetric corrective adjustment that penalises the agent requesting the most slots and rewards the agent requesting the fewest, and is designed to mitigate strategic dominance by large agents while preserving transparency and congestion sensitivity.
